Q: What are the categories for Student Visas

A: The categories for Student Visas (S) are as follows:  
- **S-1**: Higher education, exchange programs, and skill development in Ayush or Theological studies.  
- **S-2**: School-level education.  
- **S-3**: Studies in Yoga, Vedic culture, Indian music/dance, Buddhist studies, or Indian languages.  
- **S-4**: Research scholars, visiting faculty, and G-20 Talent Visa holders.  
- **S-5**: Internships in India.  
- **S-6**: For internships at Indian companies, educational institutions, NGOs, or foreign missions (as per Source 5).  

(Note: S-5 and S-6 both relate to internships, but they are distinct categories in the context provided.)  
**Citations**: Source 1 (Page 12), Source 5 (Page 31).

[2026-06-10 15:48:21]
Q: eta

A: ETA (Electronic Travel Authorization) is an authorization that allows the bearer access into India. It is **not** equivalent to an eVisa; upon entry into India by an Immigration Officer, it becomes an eVisa, which is then stamped on the passport at designated ICPs.  

- **ETA Expiry Date**: The last date allowing entry into India (per Source 2, Page 77).  
- **Visa Expiry Date**: The last date the visa remains valid (per Source 2, Page 77).  

If arrival at the immigration counter occurs **after the ETA expiry date** due to flight delays, entry may not be permitted unless a **transit visa** is obtained for extended waiting periods (per Source 5, Page 78).  

For transit purposes, a 24-hour stay in the airport is allowed for connecting flights, but longer stays require a transit visa.  

**Citations**:  
- ETA definition and eVisa distinction: Source 2, Page 77.  
- Expiry date differences: Source 2, Page 77.  
- Transit visa rules: Source 5, Page 78.

Q: i'm a afghanisthan national i would like to start business in india what are the types of visa

A: To start a business in India, as an Afghan national, you should apply for a **Business Visa**. This visa is specifically for individuals visiting India to establish or explore the possibility of setting up an industrial or business venture (Source 1, Page 26). If you are a promoter or majority shareholder of the company, the Business Visa is the correct category (Source 2, Page 39). 

If you are working for a company in India (e.g., as an employee), an **Employment Visa** would apply, but this is not relevant for starting a business. The **E-3 Visa** (Employment) is for work-related activities, including voluntary work, but not for business establishment (Source 5, Page 39). 

Always ensure compliance with immigration regulations and consult official guidelines for specific eligibility criteria.

Q: RAG system architecture

A: The RAG system architecture, as described in the documents, combines **semantic vector search** using **FAISS** with **local Large Language Models (LLMs)** via **Ollama**. The deployment follows a **lightweight local architecture** where:  
- The **client** interacts through a **browser interface**.  
- A **Flask backend server** (running on port 8003) handles **PDF ingestion** and **user query processing**.  
- The backend connects to the **Ollama AI engine** (port 11434) for generating AI-based responses.  

This setup enables efficient retrieval and generation, leveraging both document data and model intelligence. (Citations: Page 4 [Source 2], Page 8 [Source 5]).

[2026-06-10 17:50:28]
Q: PDF QA Workflow

A: The PDF QA workflow involves the following steps:  
1. **PDF Upload**: Users upload PDFs via the `/upload_pdf` endpoint, triggering the `process_pdfs()` function to extract and process document content (Source 3, Page 13).  
2. **FAISS Indexing**: `ingest.py` builds a FAISS vector database from extracted PDF content (Source 2, Page 9; Source 5, Page 3).  
3. **Query Handling**: When a chat query is received, `app.py` routes it to `rag.py`'s `ask()` function, which executes a pipeline including:  
   - **Direct QA**: Keyword-based retrieval.  
   - **Exact Entry/Section**: Precise document section matching.  
   - **Vector Search**: Semantic retrieval using FAISS embeddings.  
   - **LLM Fallback**: Generates answers if retrieval methods fail (Source 2, Page 9).  
4. **Answer Delivery**: The system returns a JSON-formatted answer via the API (Source 2, Page 9).  

This workflow ensures secure, enterprise-grade PDF-based question answering with strict validation to prevent hallucinations (Source 4, Page 3).

Q: What is an ETA

A: According to page 77, an ETA (Electronic Travel Authorization) is an authorization that allows the bearer access into the issuing country (India). It is explicitly stated that ETA is NOT an eVisa; however, when the bearer is allowed entry into India by an Immigration Officer, it becomes an eVisa, which is then stamped on the passport at designated ICPs upon production of a valid ETA.

   - Limited to 180 days in a calendar year (page 78).

Q: What are the categories for Student Visas

A: Student Visas (S) are categorized as follows:  
- **S-1**: Higher education, exchange programs, and skill development in Ayush or Theological studies.  
- **S-2**: School-level education.  
- **S-3**: Studies in Yoga, Vedic culture, Indian music/dance, Buddhist studies, or Indian languages.  
- **S-4**: Research scholars, visiting faculty, and G-20 Talent Visa holders.  
- **S-5**: Internships in India.  

This structure covers academic, cultural, and professional pursuits under the Student Visa category.

Q: Can I leave my ship to catch a flight from a nearby airport

A: Yes, you can leave your ship to catch a flight from a nearby airport, but **only with prior approval from the competent authority**. You must hold a **valid ticket for a scheduled air service** and will only be allowed to leave the ship to proceed with your journey within the permitted period. If your stay (even for the flight) exceeds the allowed time, the **Transit Visa becomes invalid**, and you’ll need to apply for a fresh one. Always confirm details with the immigration office beforehand.

Q: do i have to vaccinate to visit india

A: No, you do not necessarily need to get vaccinated to visit India if you are departing from India to a Yellow Fever endemic country. However, it is strongly advised by the Indian government for travelers leaving India for such regions to receive vaccination and carry a valid certificate. This advice applies to all passengers except infants younger than six months old.

If you are visiting an endemic area and cannot produce a valid Yellow Fever certificate, you will be placed in quarantine for up to 6 days.

Q: eta

A: The ETA, or Electronic Travel Authorization, allows you access into India. It is not an eVisa; when a bearer is allowed entry by an Immigration Officer, it becomes an eVisa. The ETA expiry date indicates the last date for which you are permitted to enter India.

Q: i'm a afghanisthan national i would like to start business in india what are the types of visa

A: If you're an Afghanistani national looking to start a business in India, you should apply for a **Business Visa**. This visa is suitable if you plan on:

- Establishing or exploring the possibility of setting up an industrial or business venture.
- Attending technical meetings, board meetings, or general discussions.
- Providing business services support or professional consultations.
- Participating in commercial sports events on contract (such as the IPL, ISL, or IBL), including acting as a coach.

You should apply for this visa rather than other types like Employment (E-3) Visa, which is applicable if you are working for free or receiving a small honorarium (up to Rs. 10,000 per month).

[2026-06-30 15:38:49] (response time: 12.69 seconds = 0 minutes 12.69 seconds)
Q: RAG system architecture

A: The **RAG** (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) system architecture at Open Technology Group is designed to answer questions directly from uploaded PDF documents. It combines semantic vector search using FAISS with local Large Language Models running through Ollama.

Key components of the RAG system include:

1. **PDF Upload**: Users can upload enterprise documents.
2. **PDF Parsing**: The text within these documents is extracted using PyPDF.
3. **Semantic Vector Search (FAISS)**: This component helps in searching and retrieving relevant information from the uploaded PDFs based on semantic similarity.
4. **Local Large Language Models (Ollama)**: These models process and generate responses to questions, leveraging the semantic context provided by FAISS.

This architecture allows for a comprehensive retrieval and generation system that can understand and respond to queries about enterprise documents effectively.

Sources:

Q: PDF QA Workflow

A: The workflow for PDF QA involves several steps:

1. **app.py** receives `/upload_pdf` → calls `ingest.py` → FAISS index is built.
2. **app.py** receives `/chat query` → calls `rag.py`'s `ask()` method → returns a JSON answer.

In the RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) process, it runs through different pipelines including Direct QA, Exact Entry, Section, Vector, and LLM fallback. 

For PDF ingestion, **ingest.py** builds FAISS index from the uploaded PDF documents.

Q: What is an ETA

A: ETA stands for Electronic Travel Authorization. It allows the bearer access into the issuing country (India). ETA is NOT eVisa. When the bearer of ETA is allowed entry into India by the Immigration Officer, it becomes eVisa. eVisa will be stamped on the Passport of the foreigner at the designated ICPs on production of valid ETA.

Q: yellow fever person allowed in india

A: Yellow fever travelers are generally not allowed entry into India unless they meet certain criteria:

1. **Infants below six months**: They are exempt from quarantine regulations and do not need a Yellow Fever certificate.
2. **Vaccination Requirement**: Travelers departing India to endemic countries must carry a valid Yellow Fever Vaccination Certificate, but the Government of India does not mandate health checks for passengers leaving for Yellow Fever endemic areas.

However, if travelers arriving in India from Yellow Fever endemic areas fail to produce a valid certificate, they may be subject to quarantine regulations. The maximum period of quarantine imposed is 6 days.

Q: Summarise this document

A: This document provides information about the Bureau of Immigration (BoI), including its establishment year, primary functions, and details on FRROs. The BoI was established in 1971 and is responsible for enforcing rules regarding foreign nationals' entry, stay, and exit in India.

There are currently 15 Foreigners Regional Registration Offices (FRROs) under the BoI, located in Ahmedabad, Amritsar, Bangalore, Calicut, Chandigarh, Chennai, Cochin, Delhi, Guwahati, Hyderabad, Jaipur, Kolkata, Lucknow, Mumbai, and Trivandrum. There are also 24 Chief Immigration Officers stationed at these FRROs.

The document outlines the process for obtaining an Indian visa if you're in a Yellow Fever endemic country. Typically, you need to show a valid vaccination certificate provided by your Indian Mission. Additionally, it mentions that biometric information must be provided before receiving a visa or registration, and this can be done through various means such as Passenger Name Record (PNR), Advance Passenger Information (API), and the Central Government's electronic portal.

The document also covers important aspects of immigration procedures in India, including submitting General Declarations to Immigration Officers at D-45 minutes before scheduled departure time. It is mandatory for carriers to submit a General Declaration in Form VIII to the Immigration Officer at this point.

Lastly, it specifies that surrendering your last Indian passport to the nearest Indian Consulate or Regional Passport Office (RPO) immediately after becoming a foreign citizen is required under the Passports Act. Keeping an Indian passport as a foreign national is illegal and considered an offense under both the Citizenship Act and the Passports Act.

Q: eta

A: ETA stands for Electronic Travel Authorization, which is an electronic permit that allows the bearer access into India. It is not equivalent to a visa; instead, it serves as a preliminary step before obtaining a visa. When the bearer of ETA is allowed entry into India by the Immigration Officer, it transforms into an eVisa. The eVisa will be stamped on the Passport at designated ICPs upon production of a valid ETA.

For more detailed information about visas and their usage in India, you can refer to FAQ 3 which explains the difference between "ETA Expiry Date" and "Visa Expiry Date."

Q: i'm a afghanisthan national i would like to start business in india what are the types of visa

A: For starting a business in India, as an Afghanistani national, you should consider applying for a **Business Visa**. Here’s why:

- You can apply if your purpose is to:
  - Establish or explore setting up an industrial or business venture.
- Attend technical meetings, board meetings, or general discussions.
- Provide business services support or professional consultations.
- Participate in commercial sports events on contract (such as the IPL, ISL, or IBL), including acting as a coach.

These activities align with the criteria for a Business Visa. However, it's important to note that you should enter India on this visa category if you are a promoter or majority shareholder/owner of a company. 

For any other types of visas like Employment (E-3) or others, they might not be suitable based on your specific purpose and status in the Indian business context.

Q: RAG system architecture

A: The **RAG** system architecture is designed with a scalable and robust structure. It integrates several key components to enhance its functionality:

1. **PDF Upload**: Users can upload enterprise documents for processing.
2. **PDF Parsing**: The uploaded PDFs are parsed using the `PyPDF` library, extracting text content.

3. **Semantic Vector Search (SvS) with FAISS**: This component uses Semantic Vector Search combined with FAISS to index and search through the extracted text efficiently.

4. **Local Large Language Models (LLMs) via Ollama**: The system leverages local LLMs running on Ollama for semantic understanding and generation of responses, enabling it to answer questions directly from the uploaded PDF documents.

This architecture ensures that the RAG system can handle various enterprise needs while maintaining flexibility and security.

Q: PDF QA Workflow

A: The **PDF QA Workflow** involves several steps in the system:

1. **Upload PDF**: Users upload a PDF file through an interface on the frontend form.
2. **Ingest.py Execution**: The uploaded PDF is processed by `ingest.py`, which extracts and prepares the document content for further processing.
3. **FAISS Index Building**: `ingest.py` triggers the process_pdfs() function, which builds a FAISS index from the extracted data to enable efficient search and retrieval of information within the PDF.

This workflow ensures that the PDF is properly indexed so it can be searched efficiently when users query for specific information using the QA system.

FAQ (Frequently Asked Questions) 
1. **What is an ETA?** An ETA is an Electronic Travel Authorization. It allows the bearer access into the issuing country (India). ETA is NOT eVisa. When the bearer of ETA is allowed entry into India by the Immigration Officer, it becomes eVisa. eVisa will be stamped on the Passport of the foreigner at the designated ICPs on production of valid ETA.
3. What is the difference between “ETA Expiry date” and “Visa Expiry Date”? 
Ans: ETA expiry date is the last date allowing bearer access into India. Visa Expiry Date is the last date permitted for the stay of a foreigner in India subjected to any stay stipulation applicable.

Q: yellow fever person allowed in india

A: Yellow Fever travelers are generally not allowed entry into India unless they meet certain criteria:

- **Infants below six months:** They are exempt from the Yellow Fever vaccination requirement and quarantine.
- **Vaccination Requirement:** Passengers who have visited or transited through Yellow Fever affected countries and fail to produce a valid certificate must carry out quarantine for up to 6 days.

- **Quarantine Exemption:** Infants younger than six months do not need to provide a Yellow Fever vaccination certificate, but they are still subject to quarantine if arriving from endemic regions without proof of vaccination.

For travelers departing India to endemic countries:
- The Government of India does not mandate health checks for passengers.
- Travelers are strongly advised to receive the Yellow Fever vaccine and carry a valid certificate before departure.

Q: Is registration required with FRRO if i have a tourist visa to india

A: A: Registration is not mandatory for tourists holding a **Tourist Visa** in India. However, it depends on other factors such as your nationality and the length of your stay. For most travelers, registration with the Foreigner’s Regional Registration Office (FRRO) is only required if your visa is valid for more than 180 days; in this case, you must register within 14 days of your arrival.

For Pakistan nationals, regardless of their visa type, registration is mandatory within 24 hours upon arrival.

Q: What does page 1 say?

A: This page provides detailed information about the Bureau of Immigration (BoI) in India. Here are the key points:

- **Establishment**: The BoI was established in 1971.
- **Primary Functions**:
  - Enforces rules regarding entry, stay, and exit of foreign nationals.
  - Acts as the authentic gateway for travel regulations and immigration services.
- **Leadership**:
  - Headed by the Commissioner of Immigration.
- **Contact Details**:
  - Name: Ms. I.B. Rani, IPS
  - Address: 7th Floor, Tower 'J', World Trade Centre (WTC), Nauroji Nagar, New Delhi - 110029
  - Telephone No: 011-26102622 (Telefax)
- **Management**:
  - Oversees immigration services across India’s International Check Posts (ICPs) through FRROs and CHIOs.
- **Services Provided for Foreign Nationals**:
  - Oversees registration services and visa-related formalities for foreign nationals residing in India, ensuring compliance with relevant Acts, Rules, and Regulations.
- **Number of Immigration Check Posts**:
  - There are currently 114 Immigration Check Posts across India, covering various routes including air, sea, land, rail, and river.

There is no information provided about the number of ICPs currently operational in India.

Q: thats fine could you please guide me in obtaining OCI Card

A: Sure, here’s a step-by-step guide to help you obtain your OCI (Overseas Citizenship of India) card:

1. **Visit the Official Portal**: Start by visiting the official OCI Services website at [https://ociservices.gov.in](https://ociservices.gov.in). This is where you can submit your application online.

2. **Submit Your Application Online**: Fill out the application form on their website, providing all necessary details and uploading required documents such as passport copy, proof of residence in India, etc.

3. **Obtain a Police Clearance Certificate (PCC)**: If you are living in India, apply for a PCC through the official portal at [https://indianfrro.gov.in](https://indianfrro.gov.in). Ensure to indicate your purpose and submit via this platform.

4. **Protected Area Permit (PAP) or Restricted Area Permit (RAP)**: After submitting your application, you might need to obtain a Protected Area Permit (PAP) or Restricted Area Permit (RAP) if you plan to visit any location designated as such by the Government of India.

5. **Review and Submit**: Review all documents carefully before final submission. Ensure everything is in order and that you have obtained all necessary permits, including PAP/RAF if required.

6. **Wait for Approval**: Once your application is submitted, it will be reviewed. You may need to provide additional information or documentation based on the review process.

Q: how to extend my stay period in india after my visa expiration

A: When extending your stay period in India after your visa expiration, you should apply for an extension through the **Foreigners Regional Registration Office (FRRO)** website at [https://indianfrro.gov.in](https://indianfrro.gov.in). 

Here are the steps to follow:

1. Ensure that your current visa is still valid.
2. Submit your request while leaving enough time for processing.
3. If your extension is not granted, you will need to apply and pay for a fresh Transit Visa.

If you do not travel through India within the validity period of your Transit Visa, the visa will lapse, and you will need to apply for a new one. Transit Visas are generally not extendable. 

It's highly recommended to apply well in advance to allow reasonable time for the FRRO/FRO to process your request. If permission to extend stay is not granted within the validity of the current visa, you must depart from India immediately after it expires.

Q: do i have to vaccinate to visit india

A: Indian Missions typically require you to show a valid vaccination certificate before issuing a visa if you are departing from a Yellow Fever endemic country. Therefore, it is strongly advised that travelers receive the Yellow Fever vaccination and carry a valid certificate for their own safety and to meet the entry requirements of your destination country.

For infants younger than six months, there is no requirement for a Yellow Fever vaccine as they are exempted by authorities in India. However, if you are not vaccinated and cannot produce a valid certificate, you will be placed in quarantine for up to 6 days upon arrival in India. This rule applies to everyone except infants under six months old.

So, yes, it is required that you get the Yellow Fever vaccine before leaving India for a Yellow Fever endemic country.

[2026-07-02 17:01:07] (response time: 12.06 seconds = 0 minutes 12.06 seconds)
Q: eta

A: For **ETA**, or Electronic Travel Authorization, it allows the bearer access into India. It is not an eVisa. When a traveler with an ETA is allowed entry by the Immigration Officer in India, their ETA becomes an eVisa. The eVisa will be stamped on the passport of the foreigner at designated ICPs (Immigration Control Points) upon production of a valid ETA.

The **ETA Expiry Date** indicates the last date for which the bearer can access India with the authorization. On the other hand, the **Visa Expiry Date** specifies the last date for which a foreigner is permitted to stay in India under any stipulated conditions related to their visa.

Q: RAG system architecture

A: The **RAG** system architecture is designed with a scalable architecture that supports future expansion and advanced enterprise features. It combines semantic vector search using **FAISS** with local Large Language Models running through **Ollama**.

Here are the core functionalities of the RAG system:

- **PDF Upload**: Allows users to upload enterprise documents.
- **PDF Parsing**: Extracts text from uploaded PDF files using **PyPDF**.

This architecture enables direct answering of questions from uploaded PDF documents.

[2026-07-02 17:02:19] (response time: 20.54 seconds = 0 minutes 20.54 seconds)
Q: PDF QA Workflow

A: The PDF QA workflow involves several steps in your system architecture:

1. **Upload PDF**:
   - Users upload a PDF file through an frontend form.
- The uploaded PDF is sent to the `/upload_pdf` endpoint using a POST request.
- The backend saves the file to disk and triggers the `process_pdfs()` function.

2. **Ingestion Process**:
   - The `ingest.py` script is called by the `/upload_pdf` endpoint.
- It builds an FAISS index from the PDFs, which helps in efficient search operations later on.

3. **Question Handling**:
   - Users can then query the system for answers using a `/chat/query` endpoint.
- The `app.py` receives this request and calls the `rag.py` script's `ask()` function.
- This function runs through different pipelines including Direct QA, Exact Entry, Section, Vector, and LLM fallback.

4. **QA Execution**:
   - Depending on the type of query (e.g., Direct QA, Exact Entry), the system uses FAISS index to find relevant documents within the PDFs.
- The `rag.py` script then processes these findings using a combination of AI techniques such as Natural Language Processing and Large Language Models.

This workflow ensures that both the ingestion of PDF files and their subsequent querying are handled efficiently and effectively.

Q: yellow fever person allowed in india

A: Yellow Fever travelers are generally not **allowed** entry into India if they do not produce a valid vaccination certificate. Here’s how the rules apply:

1. **Passengers who visited or transited through Yellow Fever affected countries and fail to produce a valid certificate are subject to quarantine regulations in India.**
   - This means that if someone has been in an area where Yellow Fever is prevalent but did not have a valid vaccine certificate, they will be quarantined for up to 6 days.

2. **Vaccination advisory for travelers departing India to endemic countries:**
   - The Government of India does not mandate health checks for passengers leaving for Yellow Fever endemic areas.
- However, travelers are strongly advised to receive the vaccination and carry a valid certificate before arriving in India.

3. **Yellow Fever certificate requirements enforced by authorities in India:**
   - Infants below six months old are exempt from needing a Yellow Fever certificate upon entry into India.
- For those older than six months, they must have a valid Yellow Fever Vaccination Certificate to enter India.

4. **Quarantine requirement for passengers without a valid certificate:**
   - Travelers arriving in India from Yellow Fever endemic areas without a valid vaccination certificate may be quarantined for up to 6 days.

In summary, while some travelers might face quarantine due to lack of certificates, the primary concern is ensuring that those entering India are vaccinated and have proper documentation.

Q: Technical specifications for biometric collection

A: The technical specifications for biometric collection in India are defined and periodically updated by the Ministry of Home Affairs according to technical specifications prescribed by the Central Government.

Here are some key points regarding the technical specifications:

1. **Biometric Information Capture**: Biometric information under the immigration system is captured according to these technical specifications.
2. **Authorized Entities**: Authorities empowered to capture biometric information include Immigration Officers, civil authorities, Registration Officers, Border Guarding Forces, Coast Guard, State authorities, Union Territory authorities, and security agencies designated by the Central Government.
3. **Fingerprints for Fewer Fingers**: For individuals with fewer than ten fingers, only available fingerprints are captured.

For the most current technical details, you can visit the Ministry of Home Affairs website at [www.mha.gov.in](http://www.mha.gov.in).

Q: what are the document requirements for exit permit

A: To obtain an Exit Permit, a foreigner is required under specific circumstances:

- **If they were issued a One-Time Travel Document or Laissez-Passer**, as mentioned in the context provided. 

For international adoptions under CARA guidelines:
- **Foreign nationals adopting an Indian child** must obtain exit permits if the child's nationality changes.
- If the child retains Indian nationality, both adoptive parents need to obtain exit permits.
- If the child acquires a foreign nationality, only one parent is required to obtain an Exit Permit.

Additionally, when leaving India for international travel:
- **A valid Indian Passport** must be carried.
- **A valid Visa for your destination country** must also be presented.

Q: thats fine could you please guide me in obtaining OCI Card

A: Sure, here’s a step-by-step guide to help you obtain your OCI (Ordinary Citizenship of India) card:

1. **Visit the Official Portal**:
   - Go to [https://ociservices.gov.in](https://ociservices.gov.in). This is where you can submit your application for registration as an OCI Cardholder.
- Make sure to review the detailed information about specific documents required for your case on this portal.

2. **Submit Your Application**:
   - Fill out the online form with all necessary details and upload the required documents.
- Ensure that all provided documents are valid and meet the requirements specified by the Government of India.

3. **Obtain a Police Clearance Certificate (PCC)**:
   - If you need a PCC, apply to the FRRO/FRO (First Information Report Officer/First Information Record Office) that covers your place of residence.
- Apply via the official portal: [https://indianfrro.gov.in](https://indianfrro.gov.in).

4. **Protected Area Permit**:
   - OCI cardholders must obtain a Protected Area Permit (PAP) or Restricted Area Permit (RAP) to visit any location designated as such by the Government of India.
- Check which areas you need access to and apply accordingly.

5. **Eligibility for Children Born Through Certain Circumstances**:
   - If your child was born through IVF, surrogacy, or live-in relationships, they are eligible for OCI registration.
- These applications are reviewed on a case-to-case basis and may require additional documentation or specific approvals.

6. **Professional and Personal Benefits of the OCI Card**:
   - The OCI card provides several professional and personal benefits, such as visa-free entry into India and other privileges.
- Review these benefits to understand how they can benefit you personally and professionally.

By following these steps, you should be able to successfully apply for your OCI card. If you encounter any issues or need further assistance, don’t hesitate to reach out to the official portals or contact the relevant authorities directly.

Q: how to extend my stay period in india after my visa expiration

A: To extend your stay period in India after your visa expiration, you should follow these steps:

1. **Check Your Visa Validity**: Ensure that your current visa has expired or is about to expire soon.

2. **Visit the FRRO Website**: Go to the [FRRO website at https://indianfrro.gov.in](). This is where you can apply for a visa extension if eligible.

3. **Submit Your Application Early**: Apply well in advance of your current visa's expiration date. The FRRO/FRO needs reasonable time to process your request.

4. **Ensure Compliance with Rules**: Make sure that all the required documents are ready and comply with the rules set by the Indian government for visa extensions.

5. **Be Prepared for Possible Denial**: If your application is not granted, you will need to depart India immediately as per the guidelines provided in the FAQ section.

6. **Plan Your Departure**: In case of a denied extension or conversion, plan your departure from India promptly after receiving notification about the denial.

By following these steps, you can manage your stay period effectively and avoid any legal issues related to overstaying in India.

Q: BOI of india

A: The Bureau of Immigration (BoI) in India was established in **1971**. 

Its primary functions include enforcing rules regarding the entry, stay, and exit of foreign nationals, acting as the authentic gateway for travel regulations and immigration services across India.

The BoI is led by the **Commissioner of Immigration**, who oversees Immigration Check Posts (ICPs) through Foreigners Regional Registration Offices (FRROs) and Chief Immigration Officers (CHIOs).

Services provided by the BoI for foreign nationals living in India include overseeing registration services and visa-related formalities, ensuring compliance with relevant Acts, Rules, and Regulations.

Currently, there are **multiple** ICPs operational in India managed by the Bureau of Immigration. 

For contact details of the Commissioner:
- **Name:** Ms. I.B. Rani, IPS
- **Office Address:** 7th Floor, Tower 'J', World Trade Centre (WTC), Nauroji Nagar, New Delhi - 110029
- **Telephone No:** 011-26102622 (Telefax)

Q: Summarise this document

A: This document provides information about the Bureau of Immigration (BoI), including its establishment year, primary functions, and details on Foreigners Regional Registration Offices (FRROs). There are 15 FRROs located across various cities such as Ahmedabad, Amritsar, Bangalore, Calicut, Chandigarh, Chennai, Cochin, Delhi, Guwahati, Hyderabad, Jaipur, Kolkata, Lucknow, Mumbai, and Trivandrum. The BoI is responsible for enforcing rules regarding the entry, stay, and exit of foreign nationals.

The document also covers visa requirements, including how to obtain an Indian visa if you are in a Yellow Fever endemic country. It mentions that biometric information must be provided by foreigners before their visa or registration can be granted. Additionally, it outlines different forms such as Passenger and Crew Manifest (Form V), Advance Passenger Information (API), and Passenger Name Record (PNR) used for travel data submission.

The document also includes details on the General Declaration in Form VIII that every carrier must submit to the Immigration Officer at D-45 minutes before scheduled departure time. Lastly, it specifies which FRROs are responsible for controlling Immigration Check Posts (ICP) at different locations such as Ahmedabad and Lucknow.

[2026-07-10 16:14:44] (response time: 26.01 seconds = 0 minutes 26.01 seconds)
Q: architecture summary

A: This document describes the **PageIndex RAG** system, an AI-powered Retrieval Augmented Generation platform designed to answer questions directly from uploaded PDF documents. The system integrates semantic vector search using FAISS with local Large Language Models through Ollama. 

The architecture of the system involves several key steps: first, data is split into chunks; second, embeddings are generated using Ollama; third, these embeddings are stored in a FAISS database for efficient similarity searches. When a user asks a question, relevant chunks are retrieved from the database and used to extract an exact answer.

Key features of this system include offline AI processing capabilities, support for PDF documents, fast semantic search, ability to provide precise answers, hybrid keyword and vector retrieval methods, low hallucination rates in responses, and localization. The document also mentions technologies such as Python for backend development and integration with Ollama for language model operations.

This system aims to offer a robust solution for extracting accurate information from PDFs using advanced AI techniques.

Q: Summarise this document

A: Let's break down the main points of this project 📄. 

**Project Overview**
The project is an Enterprise-grade PDF-based RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) AI chatbot system developed using Python, Flask, FAISS Vector Database, and Ollama Local LLM.

**Key Objectives**

* Build a lightweight enterprise AI assistant
* Achieve fast PDF-based search retrieval
* Minimize hallucination issues
* Support local offline AI execution
* Create secure document intelligence platform
* Provide accurate contextual answers

These objectives are aimed at creating a robust and efficient system for answering questions from uploaded PDF documents.

**Technology Stack**
The technology stack includes:

* **Python**: Core backend language
* **Flask**: Web framework and API server
* **FAISS**: High-speed vector similarity search
* **Ollama**: Local LLM inference engine

These technologies work together to enable fast and accurate search retrieval from PDF documents.

**System Workflow**
The complete end-to-end workflow involves:

1. PDF ingestion
2. Embedding conversion using **nomic-embed-text / mxbai-embed-large**
3. Vector similarity search using FAISS
4. Local LLM inference engine (Ollama) for response generation

This workflow enables the system to answer questions directly from uploaded PDF documents.

**Enterprise Architecture**
The project implements a complete Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) architecture, combining semantic vector search using FAISS with local Large Language Models running through Ollama.

Q: Who decides whether the visa will be granted to a UNHCR 
card holder

A: 📍 **Decision Authority:** Each application is evaluated on a case-by-case basis by the jurisdictional **FRRO (Foreigners Regional Registration Office)**.

Here's how it works:

1. The applicant submits their application electronically through the official e-FRRO portal at **indianfrro.gov.in**.
2. The FRRO will assess the documentation provided and determine the eligibility for the specific visa service requested.
3. The decision authority is the jurisdictional FRRO, which means that each case is evaluated individually based on its merits.

That's it! 📝

Q: eta ?

A: 🤔 It looks like you're asking about an **ETA**, which stands for Electronic Travel Authorization. An ETA is an electronic travel authorization that allows the bearer access into the issuing country (India). 📍

Here are some key points to keep in mind:

*   **ETA Expiry Date**: This is the last date allowing the bearer access into India.
*   **Visa Expiry Date**: This is the last date permitted for the stay of a foreigner in India, subject to any stay stipulation applicable.

If you're unsure about your ETA or have questions about your visa, I recommend checking out the FAQs provided by the In-Charge, Sh. Aravind Menon, IPS 📞 (0471-2573512) or sending an email to frrotvm@nic.in ✉️

Q: Summarise this document

A: Let's break down the main points of this document 📄.

**Project Overview**
The project aims to develop an Enterprise-grade PDF-based RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) AI chatbot system. It combines semantic vector search using FAISS with local Large Language Models running through Ollama. The system allows users to upload PDF documents, convert their content into embeddings, and store these embeddings in FAISS vectors.

**Key Objectives**
The primary goals of the project are:
• Build a lightweight enterprise AI assistant
• Achieve fast PDF-based search retrieval
• Minimize hallucination issues
• Support local offline AI execution
• Create a secure document intelligence platform
• Provide accurate contextual answers

**Technology Stack**
The technology stack includes:
• **Python** as the core backend language
• **Flask** as the web framework and API server
• **FAISS** for high-speed vector similarity search
• **Ollama** as the local LLM inference engine
• **NumPy** for vector processing

**System Workflow**
The complete end-to-end workflow involves:
1. PDF ingestion
2. Embedding conversion
3. Embedding storage in FAISS
4. AI answer delivery

**Enterprise Architecture**
The project implements a complete Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) architecture, designed to answer questions directly from uploaded PDF documents.

Q: eta

A: **ETA (Electronic Travel Authorization)**

* ETA is an Electronic Travel Authorization that allows the bearer access into the issuing country (India).
* ETA is NOT eVisa. When the bearer of ETA is allowed entry into India by the Immigration Officer, it becomes eVisa.
* eVisa will be stamped on the Passport of the foreigner at the designated ICPs on production of valid ETA.

Q: difference between ETA Expiry date and Visa Expiry Date

A: 📍 **Difference between ETA Expiry Date and Visa Expiry Date:**

| **Key Point** | **ETA Expiry Date** | **Visa Expiry Date** |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Definition | Last date allowing bearer access into India | Last date permitted for the stay of foreigner in India |
| Purpose | Allows bearer to enter India | Permits foreigner to stay in India |
| Stipulation | No stay stipulation applicable | Subjected to any stay stipulation applicable |

Q: Do I need to register with FRRO (Foreigners Regional 
Registration Officer) after arriving in India

A: 📍 **Registration Requirement:** 
Yes, you need to register with the Foreigners Regional Registration Office (FRRO) after arriving in India.

📞 **Registration Type:** 
You need to register with the FRRO based on your visa category, stay duration, and specific endorsements on your visa.

📝 **Eligible Visa Types:** 
You must register within 14 days of arrival if your visa is valid for more than 180 days and you hold one of the following visa types:
• Student Visa
• Employment Visa
• Medical Visa
• Missionary Visa

📝 **Business Visa Holders:** 
You need to register if your continuous stay in India exceeds 180 days.

📝 **Pakistani Nationals:** 
You must register with the local FRO within 24 hours of your arrival in India, regardless of your visa type.

📝 **Exemptions:** 
You are exempt from registration if you hold an Official or Diplomatic passport.

*   You must also comply with the **overtime rules**, which specify the maximum number of hours you can work in a day and the maximum number of hours you can work in a week.

[2026-10-22 10:25:06] (response time: 66.60 seconds = 1 minutes 6.60 seconds)
Q: Summarise this document

A: **Bureau of Immigration (BoI) Overview**
The Bureau of Immigration (BoI) is the primary authority in India responsible for enforcing rules regarding the entry, stay, and exit of foreign nationals. It was established in 1971 and has 15 Foreigners Regional Registration Offices (FRROs) located across the country.

**Immigration Procedures**
To enter India, foreign nationals must obtain an Indian visa and register with the FRRO in the state where they plan to stay. Biometric information must be provided to the issuing authority before the visa or registration is granted. The FRROs are responsible for collecting biometrics and ensuring compliance with immigration regulations.

**Travel Requirements and Regulations**
Foreign nationals traveling to India must comply with various regulations, including submitting Advance Passenger Information (API) within 15 minutes of arrival, and providing a General Declaration in Form VIII to the Immigration Officer 45 minutes before departure. Failure to comply with these regulations can result in penalties and fines. Additionally, foreign nationals must surrender their last Indian passport to the nearest Indian Consulate or Regional Passport Office (RPO) immediately after becoming a foreign citizen.

[2026-10-22 10:48:35] (response time: 23.81 seconds = 0 minutes 23.81 seconds)
Q: Summarise this document

A: The Bureau of Immigration (BoI) in India is the primary authority responsible for enforcing rules regarding the entry, stay, and exit of foreign nationals. The BoI was established in 1971 and has 15 Foreigners Regional Registration Offices (FRROs) located across the country. These FRROs are responsible for registering foreign nationals and ensuring they comply with immigration regulations.
To obtain an Indian visa, foreign nationals must provide biometric information to the issuing authority before the visa or registration is granted. The biometric information can be collected by specified authorities, including state or union territory authorities, passenger and crew manifest, advance passenger information, and passenger name record. Foreign nationals must submit advance passenger information within 15 minutes of "wheels-off" from the port of embarkation.
Foreign nationals must also comply with various regulations, including submitting a general declaration in form VIII to the immigration officer 45 minutes before departure. Failure to do so can result in the flight or vessel departing without clearance. Additionally, foreign nationals who become citizens of India must surrender their last Indian passport to the nearest Indian consulate or regional passport office immediately.

[2026-10-22 11:11:06] (response time: 22.74 seconds = 0 minutes 22.74 seconds)
Q: Summarise this document

A: This project is an Enterprise-grade PDF-based RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) AI chatbot system. It allows users to upload PDF documents, convert the content into embeddings, and store these embeddings in a FAISS vector database. The system combines semantic vector search using FAISS with local Large Language Models running through Ollama to answer questions directly from uploaded PDF documents.
The primary goals of this project are to build a lightweight enterprise AI assistant, achieve fast PDF-based search retrieval, minimize hallucination issues, support local offline AI execution, create a secure document intelligence platform, and provide accurate contextual answers. The business benefits of this project include improved document intelligence, enhanced search capabilities, and increased accuracy in AI-powered document analysis.
The technology stack used in this project includes Python as the core backend language, Flask as the web framework and API server, FAISS for high-speed vector similarity search, and Ollama as the local LLM inference engine. The system workflow involves PDF ingestion, vector index building, and AI answer delivery, with a complete end-to-end workflow from PDF ingestion to AI answer delivery.

[TABLE 5]
| Stage | Step | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ingestion | 1. Extract | pdfplumber extracts raw text and tables from PDF pages |
| Ingestion | 2. Section | Regex detects UPPERCASE headers to split Split document sections |
| Ingestion | 3. Chunk | Q/A, numbered, bullet, and plain section chunkers run per section |
| Ingestion | 4. Embed | Ollama generates vector embeddings for each chunk |
| Ingestion | 5. Index | FAISS IndexFlatIP stores L2-normalised vectors |
| Retrieval | 6. Query | User query is embedded using the same Embed model |
| Retrieval | 7. Vector | Top-k nearest chunks retrieved by cosine Search similarity |
| Retrieval | 8. | Keyword-scored chunks added to candidate pool Keyword Boost |
| Answer | 9. QA | Regex extracts Q/A pair answers from Extraction candidate chunks |
| Answer | 10. LLM | Ollama LLM generates final answer from Generation context window |
| Answer | 11. | Not-found patterns rejected; hallucinations Validation filtered |

[TABLE 6]
| Phase | Action |
| --- | --- |
| Index Build | Convert each chunk to float32 embedding vector |
| Index Build | L2-normalise all vectors (faiss.normalize_L2) |
| Index Build | Add to IndexFlatIP (inner product = cosine on normalised vectors) |
| Index Build | Persist as index.faiss + chunks.txt |
| Query | Embed user query with same model |
| Query | L2-normalise query vector |
| Query | Search top-k by inner product similarity (≥ 0.25 threshold) |
| Query | Merge vector results with keyword-scored candidates |

| --- | --- |

Q: difference between “ETA Expiry date” and “Visa 
Expiry Date”

A: **Difference between "ETA Expiry date" and "Visa Expiry Date"**
| **Key Points** | **ETA Expiry Date** | **Visa Expiry Date** |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Definition** | Last **date** allowing bearer access into India | Last **date** permitted for the stay of foreigner in India |
| **Purpose** | Allows bearer access into India | Permits stay in India |
| **Stipulation** | Subject to stay stipulations | Subject to stay stipulations |
| **Duration** | Can be extended | Fixed |

[2026-10-22 15:36:36] (response time: 50.02 seconds = 0 minutes 50.02 seconds)
Q: eta

A: **ETA (Electronic Travel Authorization)** 
✈️ travel 
ETA is an Electronic Travel Authorization. It allows the bearer access into the issuing country (India). ETA is NOT eVisa. When the bearer of ETA is allowed entry into India by the Immigration Officer, it becomes eVisa. eVisa will be stamped on the Passport of the foreigner at the designated ICPs on production of valid ETA.

[2026-10-22 15:40:36] (response time: 30.64 seconds = 0 minutes 30.64 seconds)
Q: RAG system architecture

A: **Overview**
📄
The RAG system architecture is composed of several layers:
• **Ingestion**: pdfplumber + Chunkers
 • Extract, clean, and split PDF content
• **Embedding**: Ollama + nomic-embed-text
 • Generate semantic vector representations
• **Storage**: FAISS IndexFlatIP
 • Store embeddings in vector database
• **Retrieval**: Vector + Keyword
 • Hybrid approach for high-recall relevant chunk retrieval
**Components**
📈
• **Ingestion Engine**: ingest.py
 • Upload Page: upload.html
 • FLASK Ingestion
 • Start Extract text from PDF
 • Store
• **RAG Engine**: rag.py
 • Search Similar Chunks
 • Fast retrieval
 • Coordinates workflows
• **Chat Page**: chat.html
 • Handles routing
 • Manages sessions

[TABLE 4]
| Module | File | Responsibility |
| --- | --- | --- |
| API Server | app.py | Flask REST API — upload and chat endpoints |
| PDF Ingestion | ingest.py | PDF parsing, chunking, and embedding generation |
| RAG Engine | rag.py | Vector search, keyword search, and LLM answer generation |
| Frontend | templates/ | HTML/CSS/JS chat and upload UI |

[TABLE 5]
| Stage | Step | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ingestion | 1. Extract | pdfplumber extracts raw text and tables from PDF pages |
| Ingestion | 2. Section | Regex detects UPPERCASE headers to split Split document sections |
| Ingestion | 3. Chunk | Q/A, numbered, bullet, and plain section chunkers run per section |
| Ingestion | 4. Embed | Ollama generates vector embeddings for each chunk |
| Ingestion | 5. Index | FAISS IndexFlatIP stores L2-normalised vectors |
| Retrieval | 6. Query | User query is embedded using the same Embed model |
| Retrieval | 7. Vector | Top-k nearest chunks retrieved by cosine Search similarity |
| Retrieval | 8. | Keyword-scored chunks added to candidate pool Keyword Boost |
| Answer | 9. QA | Regex extracts Q/A pair answers from Extraction candidate chunks |
| Answer | 10. LLM | Ollama LLM generates final answer from Generation context window |
| Answer | 11. | Not-found patterns rejected; hallucinations Validation filtered |

[TABLE 6]
| Phase | Action |
| --- | --- |
| Index Build | Convert each chunk to float32 embedding vector |
| Index Build | L2-normalise all vectors (faiss.normalize_L2) |
| Index Build | Add to IndexFlatIP (inner product = cosine on normalised vectors) |
| Index Build | Persist as index.faiss + chunks.txt |
| Query | Embed user query with same model |
| Query | L2-normalise query vector |
| Query | Search top-k by inner product similarity (≥ 0.25 threshold) |
| Query | Merge vector results with keyword-scored candidates |

[TABLE 7]
| Model | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| nomic-embed-text | Semantic embedding for retrieval |
| mxbai-embed-large | Alternative high-quality embedding model |

[TABLE 8]
| Endpoint | Port | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| api/embeddings | 11434 | Generate vector embeddings for chunks and queries |
| api/generate | 11434 | Generate natural language answers from context |

[TABLE 9]
| Model | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| llama3.2:3b | Generate AI responses (lightweight, fast) |
| Mistral | Alternative fast local answer generation model |

[TABLE 10]
| Security Feature | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| Local AI Execution | Prevent cloud data leakage — all processing on-premise |
| Context Validation | Prevent hallucination — answers sourced from PDF only |
| Restricted Prompting | LLM instructed to avoid outside knowledge |
| Controlled Retrieval | Only PDF chunks used as context — no web lookups |
| API Isolation | Flask API runs on internal network port only |

| --- | --- |

| RAG Engine | rag.py | Vector search, keyword search, and LLM answer generation |
| Frontend | templates/ | HTML/CSS/JS chat and upload UI |

[TABLE 5]
| Stage | Step | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ingestion | 1. Extract | pdfplumber extracts raw text and tables from PDF pages |
| Ingestion | 2. Section | Regex detects UPPERCASE headers to split Split document sections |
| Ingestion | 3. Chunk | Q/A, numbered, bullet, and plain section chunkers run per section |
| Ingestion | 4. Embed | Ollama generates vector embeddings for each chunk |
| Ingestion | 5. Index | FAISS IndexFlatIP stores L2-normalised vectors |
| Retrieval | 6. Query | User query is embedded using the same Embed model |
| Retrieval | 7. Vector | Top-k nearest chunks retrieved by cosine Search similarity |
| Retrieval | 8. | Keyword-scored chunks added to candidate pool Keyword Boost |
| Answer | 9. QA | Regex extracts Q/A pair answers from Extraction candidate chunks |
| Answer | 10. LLM | Ollama LLM generates final answer from Generation context window |
| Answer | 11. | Not-found patterns rejected; hallucinations Validation filtered |

[TABLE 6]
| Phase | Action |
| --- | --- |
| Index Build | Convert each chunk to float32 embedding vector |
| Index Build | L2-normalise all vectors (faiss.normalize_L2) |
| Index Build | Add to IndexFlatIP (inner product = cosine on normalised vectors) |
| Index Build | Persist as index.faiss + chunks.txt |
| Query | Embed user query with same model |
| Query | L2-normalise query vector |
| Query | Search top-k by inner product similarity (≥ 0.25 threshold) |
| Query | Merge vector results with keyword-scored candidates |

[TABLE 7]
| Model | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| nomic-embed-text | Semantic embedding for retrieval |
| mxbai-embed-large | Alternative high-quality embedding model |

[TABLE 8]
| Endpoint | Port | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| api/embeddings | 11434 | Generate vector embeddings for chunks and queries |
| api/generate | 11434 | Generate natural language answers from context |

[TABLE 9]
| Model | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| llama3.2:3b | Generate AI responses (lightweight, fast) |
| Mistral | Alternative fast local answer generation model |

[TABLE 10]
| Security Feature | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| Local AI Execution | Prevent cloud data leakage — all processing on-premise |
| Context Validation | Prevent hallucination — answers sourced from PDF only |
| Restricted Prompting | LLM instructed to avoid outside knowledge |
| Controlled Retrieval | Only PDF chunks used as context — no web lookups |
| API Isolation | Flask API runs on internal network port only |

Q: i'm a afghanisthan national i would like to start business in india what are the types of visa

A: ✈️ **Visa Options for Afghan Nationals**
As an Afghan national, you have several visa options to start a business in India:
* **LTV (Long Term Visa)**: Available for Afghan nationals, this visa is for a specific period and can be extended. It's suitable for entrepreneurs who want to establish a business in India.
* **X-4: Specific Stay Visa**: This visa is specifically for Afghan nationals and allows for a specific stay in India. It's not a business visa, but it can be used as a stepping stone to obtain other types of visas.
* **X-Misc: Miscellaneous Visa**: This visa is for legitimate purposes not covered by other categories. It's a catch-all visa that can be used for various business-related activities.
**Important Note**: You'll need to apply for a specific visa category and meet the required eligibility criteria. It's recommended to consult the official government website or contact the Indian embassy/consulate in Afghanistan for the most up-to-date information and guidance.

Q: Can I leave my ship to catch a flight from a nearby airport

A: ✅ **Can I leave my ship to catch a flight from a nearby airport?**

Yes, but only with prior approval from the competent authority. You must hold a valid ticket for a scheduled air service and will only be allowed to leave the ship to proceed directly to the designated airport.

Q: eta

A: 📘 **What is an ETA?**

ETA is an Electronic Travel Authorization. It allows the bearer access into the issuing country (India). ETA is NOT eVisa. When the bearer of ETA is allowed entry into India by the Immigration Officer, it becomes eVisa. eVisa will be stamped on the Passport of the foreigner at the designated ICPs on production of valid ETA.

😊 Anything else you'd like to know?

Q: difference between “ETA Expiry date” and “Visa 
Expiry Date”

A: 📘 **What is the difference between “ETA Expiry date” and “Visa Expiry Date”?**

ETA expiry date is the last date allowing bearer access into India. Visa Expiry Date is the last date permitted for the stay of foreigner in India subjected to any stay stipulation applicable. 3.

Q: eta

A: 📘 **What is an ETA?**

ETA is an Electronic Travel Authorization. It allows the bearer access into the issuing country (India). ETA is NOT eVisa. When the bearer of ETA is allowed entry into India by the Immigration Officer, it becomes eVisa. eVisa will be stamped on the Passport of the foreigner at the designated ICPs on production of valid ETA.

😊 Anything else you'd like to know?

Q: What are the categories for Student Visas

A: 📘 **What are the categories for Student Visas (S)?**

Student visas are categorized based on the level and type of study:
- S-1: Higher education, exchange programs, and skill development in Ayush or Theological studies.
- S-2: School-level education.
- S-3: Studies in Yoga, Vedic culture, Indian music/dance, Buddhist studies, or Indian languages.
- S-4: Research scholars, visiting faculty, and G-20 Talent Visa holders.
- S-5: For individuals undertaking an Internship in India.

😊 Anything else you'd like to know?

[2026-11-03 11:04:03] (response time: 45.51 seconds = 0 minutes 45.51 seconds)
Q: Summarise this document

A: **Overview of the RAG System**
The RAG System is an Enterprise-grade PDF-based RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) AI chatbot system developed using Python, Flask, FAISS Vector Database, and Ollama Local LLM. The system allows users to upload PDF documents, convert PDF content into embeddings, and store embeddings in FAISS vector database.
**Core Functionalities and Objectives**
The primary goals of the RAG System are to build a lightweight enterprise AI assistant, achieve fast PDF-based search retrieval, minimize hallucination issues, support local offline AI execution, create a secure document intelligence platform, and provide accurate contextual answers. The system aims to achieve these objectives by combining semantic vector search using FAISS with local Large Language Models running through Ollama.
**System Workflow and Architecture**
The complete end-to-end workflow from PDF ingestion to AI answer delivery involves the following stages:
1. Ingestion: Extract raw text and tables from PDF pages using pdfplumber.
2. Section: Split the document into sections using Regex.
3. Chunk: Chunk the document into Q/A, numbered, bullet, and plain sections.
The system is built using a Flask-based backend, where app.py acts as the main controller for handling user requests.
**Why These Technologies?**
The RAG System uses FAISS for ultra-fast vector similarity search, Ollama for local offline AI execution with zero cloud cost and full data privacy, and nomic-embed-text for state-of-the-art semantic understanding for retrieval quality. Mistral / llama3.2:3b is used for fast and low-latency response generation with minimal resources. Flask is used for lightweight backend APIs for upload and chat functionality.
Hope that helps! 😊

[2026-11-03 15:58:04] (response time: 26.41 seconds = 0 minutes 26.41 seconds)
Q: Summarise this document

A: **Overview of the RAG System**
The RAG System is an Enterprise-grade PDF-based RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) AI chatbot system developed using Python, Flask, FAISS Vector Database, and Ollama Local LLM. The system allows users to upload PDF documents, convert PDF content into embeddings, and store embeddings in FAISS vector database.
**Core Functionalities and Objectives**
The primary goals of the RAG System are to build a lightweight enterprise AI assistant, achieve fast PDF-based search retrieval, minimize hallucination issues, support local offline AI execution, create a secure document intelligence platform, and provide accurate contextual answers. The system aims to achieve these objectives by combining semantic vector search using FAISS with local Large Language Models running through Ollama.
**System Workflow and Architecture**
The complete end-to-end workflow from PDF ingestion to AI answer delivery involves the following stages: 
1. Ingestion: pdfplumber extracts raw text and tables from PDF pages, regex detects UPPERCASE headers to split document sections, and chunkers run per section. 
2. Section: The document is split into sections based on the UPPERCASE headers. 
3. Chunk: The system chunks the document into Q/A, numbered, bullet, and plain sections. 
4. FAISS Vector Database Workflow: The system stores embeddings in FAISS vector database for fast vector similarity search. 
5. Ollama Integration: The system uses Ollama Local LLM for fast, low-latency response generation with minimal resources. 
6. Flask Backend APIs: The system uses Flask for lightweight backend APIs for upload and handling user requests.

[2026-11-03 17:33:20] (response time: 6.15 seconds = 0 minutes 6.15 seconds)
Q: Summarise this document

A: இந்த ஆவணம் பேஜ்இன்டெக்ஸ் ஆர்ஏஜி (PageIndex RAG) என்ற நிறுவன அளவிலான பிடிஎஃப் (PDF) அடிப்படையிலான ஆர்டிஜே (RAG) செயற்கை நுண்ணறிவு சாட்பாட் அமைப்பைப் பற்றி விளக்குகிறது. இது பைதான் (Python), ஃப்ளாஸ்க் (Flask), ஃபேஸ் (FAISS) வெக்டர் டேட்டாபேஸ் மற்றும் ஓல்லாமா (Ollama) லோக்கல் எல்எல்எம் (LLM) ஆகியவற்றைப் பயன்படுத்தி உருவாக்கப்பட்டுள்ளது.
பயனர்கள் பிடிஎஃப் ஆவணங்களை பதிவேற்றலாம், அவற்றின் உள்ளடக்கத்தை வெக்டர்களாக மாற்றி ஃபேஸ் டேட்டாபேஸில் சேமிக்கலாம். பின்னர், பயனரின் கேள்விகளுக்கு அந்த ஆவணங்களில் இருந்து துல்லியமான பதில்களை வழங்க இந்த அமைப்பு உதவுகிறது.
இந்த அமைப்பின் முக்கிய குறிக்கோள்கள்:
• இலகுரக நிறுவன செயற்கை நுண்ணறிவு உதவியாளரை உருவாக்குவது
• விரைவான பிடிஎஃப் அடிப்படையிலான தேடல் மீட்பை அடைவது
• பிரமை எதிர்பார்ப்புகளை குறைப்பது
• உள்ளூர் ஆஃப்லைன் செயற்கை நுண்ணறிவு செயல்பாட்டை ஆதரிப்பது
• பாதுகாப்பான ஆவண நுண்ணறிவு தளத்தை உருவாக்குவது
இதன் தொழில்நுட்ப அடுக்கு: ஃப்ளாஸ்க் (Flask) சுவாரஸ்யமான பின்புற API-களுக்காக, ஃபேஸ் (FAISS) வெக்டர் ஒற்றுமை தேடலுக்கு, ஓல்லாமா (Ollama) உள்ளூர் LLM-களுக்கு, nomic-embed-text உள்ளடக்க புரிதலுக்காக, மற்றும் Mistral/llama3.2:3b விரைவான பதிலளிப்பிற்காக.
ஆவணங்களை உள்ளிடுதல், PDF உள்ளடக்கத்தை பிரித்தெடுத்தல் மற்றும் பிரித்தல், மற்றும் பயனர் கேள்விகளுக்கு பதிலளிப்பதற்கான படிகள் இதில் அடங்கும். இந்த அமைப்பு ஆஃப்லைனில் இயங்கும் வசதியுடன், தரவு தனியுரிமை மற்றும் பாதுகாப்பை வலியுறுத்துகிறது.
நம்பிக்கை கொள்ளவும்! 😊

Q: Authorities authorized to collect biometric information in India

A: நீங்கள் இந்தியாவிற்குள் ஒரு வெளிநாட்டவராக அடையாளம் காணப்பட்டால், பின்வரும் அதிகாரிகள் மத்திய அரசின் எலக்ட்ரானிக் போர்ட்டலில் உங்கள் பயோமெட்ரிக்ஸைப் பெறலாம்:
* 📍 **குறிப்பிட்ட மாநில அல்லது யூனியன் பிராந்திய அதிகாரிகள்**
* 📍 **சிவில் அதிகாரிகள் அல்லது பதிவு அதிகாரிகள்**

Q: What are the categories for Student Visas

A: 📘 **What are the categories for Student Visas (S)?**

Student visas are categorized based on the level and type of study:
- S-1: Higher education, exchange programs, and skill development in Ayush or Theological studies.
- S-2: School-level education.
- S-3: Studies in Yoga, Vedic culture, Indian music/dance, Buddhist studies, or Indian languages.
- S-4: Research scholars, visiting faculty, and G-20 Talent Visa holders.
- S-5: For individuals undertaking an Internship in India.

😊 Anything else you'd like to know?

The following authorities are authorized to collect biometric information from foreigners in India:

* No mandatory health check required by the Indian government for departure.

A: இந்த ஆவணம் தேசிய தகவல் தொழில்நுட்ப மையத்தில் (என்ஐசி) உள்ள ஓபன் டெக்னாலஜி குரூப் (ஓடிஜி) உருவாக்கிய செயற்கை நுண்ணறிவு சக்தியுள்ள பிடிஎஃப் கேள்வி பதில் தளமான பேஜ்இண்டெக்ஸ் ஆர்ஏஜி-ஐ விவரிக்கிறது. ✅ இது ஒரு enterprise-grade system, இது பயனர்கள் PDF ஆவணங்களைப் பதிவேற்றம் செய்யவும், தங்கள் உள்ளடக்கத்தை உட்பொதிப்புகளாக மாற்றவும், விரைவான, துல்லியமான, ஆஃப்லைன் செயற்கை நுண்ணறிவு இயக்கப்படும் பதில்களுக்காக FAISS வெக்டர் தரவுத்தளத்தில் சேமிக்கவும் அனுமதிக்கிறது.
பாதுகாப்பான ஆவண நுண்ணறிவு தீர்வை வழங்குவதற்காக, இந்த மேடையில் பைதான், ஃப்ளாஸ்க், எஃப்ஏஐஎஸ்எஸ் மற்றும் ஓல்லாமா லோக்கல் எல்எல்எம் ஆகியவை பயன்படுத்தப்பட்டுள்ளன. 📄 దీని ప్రధాన క్రియాత్మకతలు PDF ల నుండి text మరియు tables ను సంగ్రహించడం, re.gex ఉపయోగించి పత్రాలను విభాగాలుగా విభజించడం, కంటెంట్ ను ముక్కలుగా విభజించడం మరియు స్థానిక పెద్ద భాషా నమూనాలతో సెమాంటిక్ వెక్టర్ శోధనను కలపడం ద్వారా సందర్భోచిత సమాధానాలను రూపొందించడం వంటివి. முதன்மை குறிக்கோள்கள் ஒரு இலகுரக செயற்கை நுண்ணறிவு உதவியாளரை உருவாக்குவது, விரைவான PDF-அடிப்படையிலான தேடலை அடைவது, மாயத்தோற்றங்களைக் குறைப்பது, ஆஃப்லைன் செயல்படுத்தலுக்கு ஆதரவளிப்பது மற்றும் தரவு தனியுரிமையை உறுதிப்படுத்துவது.
இந்த அமைப்பு ஒரு Flask-அமைக்கப்பட்ட பின்புறத்தால் நிர்வகிக்கப்படும் முழுமையான இறுதி-டு-எண்ட் பணிப்பாய்வைப் பின்பற்றுகிறது, அங்கு `app.py` பயனர் கோரிக்கைகளை கையாளுகிறது மற்றும் செயலாக்கத்திற்காக `ingest.py` க்கு கோப்புகளை அனுப்புகிறது. 💡 RAG குழாய்வழி `pdfplumber` மூலம் பிரித்தெடுப்பது, பிரிவு பிளவு, பல்வேறு முறைகளுடன் பகுதி பிரித்தல் மற்றும் இறுதியாக, ஓலாமா வழியாக மிஸ்ட்ரல் / லாமா3.2:3b போன்ற மாதிரிகளைப் பயன்படுத்தி பதில் உருவாக்கம் ஆகியவற்றை உள்ளடக்கியது.

[TABLE 5]
| Stage | Step | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ingestion | 1. Extract | pdfplumber extracts raw text and tables from PDF pages |
| Ingestion | 2. Section | Regex detects UPPERCASE headers to split Split document sections |
| Ingestion | 3. Chunk | Q/A, numbered, bullet, and plain section chunkers run per section |
| Ingestion | 4. Embed | Ollama generates vector embeddings for each chunk |
| Ingestion | 5. Index | FAISS IndexFlatIP stores L2-normalised vectors |
| Retrieval | 6. Query | User query is embedded using the same Embed model |
| Retrieval | 7. Vector | Top-k nearest chunks retrieved by cosine Search similarity |
| Retrieval | 8. | Keyword-scored chunks added to candidate pool Keyword Boost |
| Answer | 9. QA | Regex extracts Q/A pair answers from Extraction candidate chunks |
| Answer | 10. LLM | Ollama LLM generates final answer from Generation context window |
| Answer | 11. | Not-found patterns rejected; hallucinations Validation filtered |

[TABLE 6]
| Phase | Action |
| --- | --- |
| Index Build | Convert each chunk to float32 embedding vector |
| Index Build | L2-normalise all vectors (faiss.normalize_L2) |
| Index Build | Add to IndexFlatIP (inner product = cosine on normalised vectors) |
| Index Build | Persist as index.faiss + chunks.txt |
| Query | Embed user query with same model |
| Query | L2-normalise query vector |
| Query | Search top-k by inner product similarity (≥ 0.25 threshold) |
| Query | Merge vector results with keyword-scored candidates |

| Model | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| nomic-embed-text | Semantic embedding for retrieval |
| mxbai-embed-large | Alternative high-quality embedding model |

| --- | --- |
| llama3.2:3b | Generate AI responses (lightweight, fast) |

| Controlled Retrieval | Only PDF chunks used as context — no web lookups |

Q: Summarise this document

A: இந்த ஆவணம் தேசிய தகவல் தொழில்நுட்ப மையத்தில் (என்ஐசி) உள்ள ஓபன் டெக்னாலஜி குரூப் (ஓடிஜி) உருவாக்கிய செயற்கை நுண்ணறிவு சக்தியுள்ள பிடிஎஃப் கேள்வி பதில் தளமான பேஜ்இண்டெக்ஸ் ஆர்ஏஜி-ஐ விவரிக்கிறது. ✅
இது பைதான், ஃப்ளாஸ்க், FAISS வெக்டர் டேட்டாபேஸ் மற்றும் ஒல்லாமா லோக்கல் LLM ஆகியவற்றைக் கொண்டு உருவாக்கப்பட்ட ஒரு எண்டர்பிரைஸ்-கிரேட் ரெட்ரீவல்-ஆக்மென்டட் ஜெனரேஷன் (RAG) AI சாட்போட் ஆகும். இந்த அமைப்பு பயனர்கள் PDF ஆவணங்களைப் பதிவேற்றவும், அவற்றின் உள்ளடக்கத்தை உட்பொதிப்புகளாக மாற்றவும், பதிவேற்றப்பட்ட கோப்புகளிலிருந்து நேரடியாக வேகமான, ஆஃப்லைனில் மற்றும் துல்லியமான AI-உருவாக்கப்பட்ட பதில்களைப் பெற FAISS இல் சேமிக்கவும் அனுமதிக்கிறது.
இந்தத் திட்டம் ஒரு இலகுரக நிறுவன செயற்கை நுண்ணறிவு உதவியாளரை உருவாக்க இலக்கு வைத்துள்ளது, இது விரைவான PDF-அடிப்படையிலான தேடல் மீட்பை அடைகிறது, மாயத்தோற்றத்தைக் குறைக்கிறது, உள்ளூர் ஆஃப்லைன் செயற்கை நுண்ணறிவு செயல்பாட்டை ஆதரிக்கிறது மற்றும் துல்லியமான சூழல்சார் பதில்களுடன் கூடிய பாதுகாப்பான ஆவண நுண்ணறிவு தளத்தை வழங்குகிறது. அதிவேக நோய்ப்பரப்பி ஒற்றுமை தேடலுக்கான FAISS, பூஜ்ஜிய கிளவுட் செலவு மற்றும் முழு தரவு தனியுரிமை, சொற்பொருள் புரிதலுக்கான நேர்மறை-உட்பொதி-உரை, விரைவான குறைந்த-தாமத பதில்களுக்கான மிஸ்ட்ரல் / லாமா3.2:3b, மற்றும் இலகுரக பின்புற ஏபிஐ-களுக்கான ஃப்ளாஸ்க் ஆகியவை தேர்ந்தெடுக்கப்பட்ட முக்கிய தொழில்நுட்பங்களில் அடங்கும். பணிச்சுழற்சி PDF உள்ளீடு, உரை பிரித்தெடுத்தல், பிரிவு பிளவு, பகுதி பிளவு, திசையன் குறியீட்டு மற்றும் வரையறுக்கப்பட்ட ஆர்ஏஜி குழாய் மூலம் பதில் உருவாக்கம் ஆகியவற்றை உள்ளடக்கியது.
உதவி செய்யும் என்று நம்புகிறேன்! 😊

[TABLE 5]
| Stage | Step | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ingestion | 1. Extract | pdfplumber extracts raw text and tables from PDF pages |
| Ingestion | 2. Section | Regex detects UPPERCASE headers to split Split document sections |
| Ingestion | 3. Chunk | Q/A, numbered, bullet, and plain section chunkers run per section |
| Ingestion | 4. Embed | Ollama generates vector embeddings for each chunk |
| Ingestion | 5. Index | FAISS IndexFlatIP stores L2-normalised vectors |
| Retrieval | 6. Query | User query is embedded using the same Embed model |
| Retrieval | 7. Vector | Top-k nearest chunks retrieved by cosine Search similarity |
| Retrieval | 8. | Keyword-scored chunks added to candidate pool Keyword Boost |
| Answer | 9. QA | Regex extracts Q/A pair answers from Extraction candidate chunks |
| Answer | 10. LLM | Ollama LLM generates final answer from Generation context window |
| Answer | 11. | Not-found patterns rejected; hallucinations Validation filtered |

[TABLE 6]
| Phase | Action |
| --- | --- |
| Index Build | Convert each chunk to float32 embedding vector |
| Index Build | L2-normalise all vectors (faiss.normalize_L2) |
| Index Build | Add to IndexFlatIP (inner product = cosine on normalised vectors) |
| Index Build | Persist as index.faiss + chunks.txt |
| Query | Embed user query with same model |
| Query | L2-normalise query vector |
| Query | Search top-k by inner product similarity (≥ 0.25 threshold) |
| Query | Merge vector results with keyword-scored candidates |

| Model | Purpose |
| --- | --- |

Q: What are the categories for Student Visas

A: 📘 மாணவர் விசாவிற்கு (எஸ்) என்னென்ன வகைகள் உள்ளன?

மாணவர் விசாவிற்கு படிப்பு நிலை மற்றும் படிப்பின் வகையின் அடிப்படையில் வகைப்படுத்தப்படுகிறது:
- எஸ்-1: உயர்கல்வி, பரிமாற்றத் திட்டங்கள், ஆயுஷ் அல்லது இறையியல் படிப்புகளில் திறன் மேம்பாடு.
- S-2: பள்ளி அளவிலான கல்வி.
- எஸ்-3: யோகா, வேத கலாச்சாரம், இந்திய இசை/நடனம், பௌத்த ஆய்வுகள் அல்லது இந்திய மொழிகள் ஆகியவற்றில் படிப்புகள்.
- S-4: ஆராய்ச்சி மாணவர்கள், வருகைதரு பேராசிரியர்கள் மற்றும் G-20 திறமைவாய்ப்பு விசா வைத்திருப்பவர்கள்.
- S-5: இந்தியாவில் இன்டர்ன்ஷிப் மேற்கொள்ளும் தனிநபர்களுக்கு.

- நீங்கள் விமானத்திலோ கடல்வழியாகவோ மஞ்சள் காய்ச்சல் பாதிப்பு உள்ள நாடுகளிலிருந்து வந்திருந்தால் அல்லது அங்கு கடந்து சென்றிருந்தால், இந்தியாவிற்குள் நுழைய உங்களுக்கு செல்லுபடியாகும் மஞ்சள் காய்ச்சல் தடுப்பூசிச் சான்றிதழ் தேவைப்படும்.
- நீங்கள் ஒரு நோய்த்தொற்று உள்ள நாட்டிற்குச் செல்ல விரும்பினால், இந்திய அரசு பயணத்திற்கு முன்னர் கட்டாயமாக உடல்நலப் பரிசோதனை மேற்கொள்வதில்லை, ஆனால் தடுப்பூசி போட்டுக் கொண்டு, உங்கள் இலக்கு நாட்டிற்குள் நுழைவதற்கான தேவைகளை பூர்த்தி செய்ய ஒரு சான்றிதழை எடுத்துச் செல்வது நல்லது.
உதவி செய்யும் என்று நம்புகிறேன்! 😊

[2026-11-10 17:05:34] (response time: 0.00 seconds = 0 minutes 0.00 seconds)
Q: eta

A: 📘 ETA என்றால் என்ன?

ETA என்பது ஒரு மின்னணு பயண அங்கீகாரம். இது, அதை வழங்கும் நாட்டிற்குள் (இந்தியா) பயணியர் செல்ல அனுமதிக்கிறது. ETA என்பது விசா அல்ல. இடிஏ-ஐ ஏந்திச் செல்பவர் இந்தியாவிற்குள் நுழைய குடியேற்ற அதிகாரியால் அனுமதிக்கப்பட்டால், அது இவிசா எனப்படும். செல்லுபடியாகும் ETA வழங்கி, குறிப்பிட்ட ICP-களில் வெளிநாட்டவர் வந்தவுடன் அவர்களது பாஸ்போர்ட்டில் இ-விசா முத்திரையிடப்படும்.

😊 வேறு ஏதேனும் அறிய விரும்புகிறீர்களா?

Q: i'm a afghanisthan national i would like to start business in india what are the types of visa

A: இந்தியாவில் ஒரு வணிகத்தைத் தொடங்க விரும்பும் ஒரு ஆப்கானிஸ்தான் நாட்டு குடிமகனுக்கு, பொருத்தமான விசா வகை எக்ஸ்-4 ஆகும், இது ஒரு குறிப்பிட்ட தங்கியிருப்பு விசா. எனினும், எக்ஸ்-4 விசா குறிப்பிட்ட காலத்திற்கு மட்டுமே செல்லுபடியாகும் என்பதையும், அது தொழில்முறை அல்லது வணிக நடவடிக்கைகளுக்குப் பொருந்தாது என்பதையும் தயவுசெய்து கவனத்தில் கொள்ளவும்.
வணிகத்தைத் தொடங்க, நீங்கள் வணிக விசாவுக்காக விண்ணப்பிக்க வேண்டும். ✅

Q: What are the categories for Student Visas

A: 📘 மாணவர் விசாவிற்கு (எஸ்) என்னென்ன பிரிவுகள் உள்ளன?

மாணவர் விசாவிற்கு படிப்பு நிலை மற்றும் படிப்பின் வகையின் அடிப்படையில் வகைப்படுத்தப்படுகிறது:
- எஸ்-1: உயர்கல்வி, பரிமாற்றத் திட்டங்கள், ஆயுஷ் அல்லது இறையியல் படிப்புகளில் திறன் மேம்பாடு.
- S-2: பள்ளி அளவிலான கல்வி.
- எஸ்-3: யோகா, வேத கலாச்சாரம், இந்திய இசை/நடனம், பௌத்த ஆய்வுகள் அல்லது இந்திய மொழிகள் ஆகியவற்றில் படிப்புகள்.
- S-4: ஆராய்ச்சி மாணவர்கள், வருகைதரு பேராசிரியர்கள் மற்றும் G-20 திறமைவாய்ப்பு விசா வைத்திருப்பவர்கள்.
- S-5: இந்தியாவில் இன்டர்ன்ஷிப் மேற்கொள்ளும் தனிநபர்களுக்கு.

Q: what is eta ?

A: 📘 ETA என்றால் என்ன?

ETA என்பது ஒரு மின்னணு பயண அங்கீகாரம். இது, அதை வழங்கும் நாட்டிற்குள் (இந்தியா) அதை எடுத்துச் செல்பவருக்கு அணுகலை வழங்குகிறது. ETA என்பது விசா அல்ல. இடிஏ-ஐ ஏற்கும் அதிகாரி ஒருவர் அதை இந்தியாவிற்குள் நுழைய அனுமதிக்கும் போது, அது இவிசா எனப்படும். செல்லுபடியாகும் ETA வழங்கி, குறிப்பிட்ட ICP-களில் வெளிநாட்டவர் வந்தவுடன் அவர்களது பாஸ்போர்ட்டில் இ-விசா முத்திரையிடப்படும்.

😊 வேறு ஏதேனும் அறிய விரும்புகிறீர்களா?

Q: i'm a afghanisthan national i would like to start business in india what are the types of visa

A: இந்தியாவில் ஒரு வணிகத்தைத் தொடங்க விரும்பும் ஒரு ஆப்கானிஸ்தான் நாட்டு குடிமகனுக்கு, தொடர்புடைய விசா வகைகள்:
- ✈️ எக்ஸ்-4: ஆப்கானிய நாட்டு மக்களுக்கு குறிப்பிட்ட காலத்திற்கு வழங்கப்படும் ஒரு விசாவை.
- 💼 வணிக விசா: நீங்கள் ஒரு வணிகத்தைத் தொடங்க விரும்புவதால், நீங்கள் வணிக விசாவுக்கு விண்ணப்பிக்க வேண்டும்.
⚠️ உங்கள் நோக்கம் தொழில்முறை சார்ந்ததாக இருந்தால், நீங்கள் வேலைவாய்ப்பு அல்லது வணிக விசாவுக்கு விண்ணப்பிக்க வேண்டும், ஏனெனில் எக்ஸ்-1 விசா வேலை அல்லது வணிகத்திற்காக அல்ல.
உதவி செய்யும் என்று நம்புகிறேன்! 😊

Q: விசா வகைகள்

A: உங்கள் ஆவணங்களில் குறிப்பிடப்பட்டுள்ள விசா வகைகள் இவைதான்:
- வேலைவாய்ப்பு விசா: இந்தியாவில் பணிபுரிய விரும்பும் வெளிநாட்டவர்களுக்கு இது தேவைப்படுகிறது; இது பொதுவாக உயர் திறன் அல்லது தகுதி வாய்ந்த தொழில் வல்லுநர்களுக்கு வழங்கப்படுகிறது. ✅
- சுற்றுலா விசா (டி): இரண்டு துணை வகைகளில் கிடைக்கிறது:
 - டி-1: சுற்றுலா, பொழுதுபோக்கு, நண்பர்கள் அல்லது உறவினர்களைச் சந்தித்தல், குறுகிய கால யோகா அல்லது 6 மாதங்களுக்குள் முறையான பட்டம் பெறாத படிப்புகள்.
 - T-2: மலையேற்றப் பயணங்களில் பங்கேற்கும் வெளிநாட்டு குடிமக்களுக்கு குறிப்பாக. 📄
- வணிக விசா மற்றும் மாணவர் விசா: பிற விசா வகைகளில், விசா வைத்திருப்பவர்கள் (பாகிஸ்தான் நாட்டு குடிமக்கள் தவிர) சுற்றுலா நடவடிக்கைகளில் ஈடுபடலாம். ✈️
உதவி செய்யும் என்று நம்புகிறேன்! 😊

Q: What is the capital of Tamil Nadu?

A: தமிழ்நாட்டின் தலைநகரம் சென்னை! ✅

- 📞 தொடர்பு: 044-29862069 | poechennai1@mea.gov.in
- அதிகார வரம்பு: தமிழ்நாடு மற்றும் புதுச்சேரி.

Q: What is "Emigration Clearance" and who needs it?

A: 📘 "குடியேற்ற அனுமதி" என்றால் என்ன, யாருக்கு அது தேவை?

குடியேற்றச் சட்டம், 1983இன் படி குடியேற்ற அனுமதி ஒரு சட்டத் தேவை. வேலைவாய்ப்புக்காக குறிப்பிட்ட நாடுகளுக்கு பயணம் செய்யும் இந்திய பாஸ்போர்ட் வைத்திருப்பவர்களுக்கு இது கட்டாயம்.

[2026-11-12 11:43:49] (response time: 4.88 seconds = 0 minutes 4.88 seconds)
Q: விசா வகைகள்

A: உங்கள் ஆவணங்களில் உள்ள விசா வகைகள் இவைதான்:
- எக்ஸ் விசா (இணைப்புகள்): குறிப்பிட்ட சூழ்நிலைகளுக்கு, இந்திய வம்சாவளியைச் சேர்ந்த தனிநபர்களுக்கு (பிஐஓ) மற்றும் அவர்களின் வெளிநாட்டு வாழ்க்கைத் துணைகள்/குழந்தைகளுக்கு எக்ஸ்-1, ஆரோville, அரவிந்தோ ஆசிரமம் அல்லது புத்த மடாலயங்கள் போன்ற அங்கீகரிக்கப்பட்ட அமைப்புகளில் சேருபவர்களுக்கு எக்ஸ்-2, ஆப்கானிஸ்தான் நாட்டு மக்களுக்கு எக்ஸ்-4. மற்ற பிரிவுகளில் அடங்காத எந்தவொரு சட்டபூர்வமான நோக்கத்திற்காகவும் உரை மற்றும் X-Misc.
- டி விசா (சுற்றுலா): சுற்றுலா, பொழுதுபோக்கு, நண்பர்கள்/சகோதரிகளைச் சந்தித்தல், குறுகிய கால யோகா அல்லது சாதாரண படிப்புகள் (6 மாதங்களுக்குள்) மற்றும் மலையேற்றப் பயணங்களில் பங்கேற்கும் வெளிநாட்டு குடிமக்களுக்கு டி-2 விசா எனப் பிரிக்கப்பட்டுள்ளது.
- எஸ் விசா (மாணவர்): உயர் கல்வி, பரிமாற்றத் திட்டங்கள், ஆயுஷ் அல்லது இறையியல் படிப்புகள், பள்ளி அளவிலான கல்வி, யோகா, வேத கலாச்சாரம், இந்திய இசை/நடனம், புத்த ஆய்வுகள் அல்லது இந்திய மொழிகள் ஆகியவற்றில் திறன் மேம்பாடு, எஸ்-2 பள்ளி அளவிலான கல்வி, எஸ்-3 யோகா, வேத கலாச்சாரம், இந்திய இசை/நடனம், பௌத்த ஆய்வுகள் அல்லது இந்திய மொழிகள் ஆகியவற்றில் எஸ்-4 ஆராய்ச்சி அறிஞர்கள், வருகைதரு பேராசிரியர்கள் ஆகியோருக்கான எஸ்-1 அடங்கும். மேலும், G-20 விசுவாசிகள், இந்தியாவில் பயிற்சிப் பயிலுரிமை மேற்கொள்பவர்கள் (S-5) ஆகியோருக்கும் இந்த விசா வழங்கப்படும்.
- எஃப் விசா: பெரும்பாலான விசா பிரிவுகளின் சார்பாக பயிலும் மாணவர்களுக்கு.
- D-1X முதல் D-5X வரை: குறிப்பாக இராஜதந்திர (D) விசா வைத்திருப்பவர்களின் சார்பாளர்களுக்கு.
- O-1X முதல் O-4X வரை: குறிப்பாக அதிகாரப்பூர்வ (O) விசா வைத்திருப்பவர்களின் சார்பாளர்களுக்கு.
உதவி செய்யும் என்று நம்புகிறேன்! 😊

Q: what is eta ?

A: 📘 ETA என்றால் என்ன?

ETA என்பது ஒரு மின்னணு பயண அங்கீகாரம். இது, அதை வழங்கும் நாட்டிற்குள் (இந்தியா) பயணிக்கும் நபருக்கு அனுமதி அளிக்கிறது. ETA என்பது விசா அல்ல. இடிஏ-ஐ எடுத்துச் செல்பவர் இந்தியாவிற்குள் நுழைய குடியேற்ற அதிகாரியால் அனுமதிக்கப்பட்டால், அது இவிசா எனப்படும். செல்லுபடியாகும் ETA வழங்கி, குறிப்பிட்ட ICP-களில் வெளிநாட்டவர் வந்தவுடன் அவர்களது பாஸ்போர்ட்டில் இ-விசா முத்திரை பதிக்கப்படும்.

😊 வேறு ஏதேனும் அறிய விரும்புகிறீர்களா?

[2026-11-12 11:46:25] (response time: 0.00 seconds = 0 minutes 0.00 seconds)
Q: what is eta ?

A: 📘 **What is an ETA?**

ETA is an Electronic Travel Authorization. It allows the bearer access into the issuing country (India). ETA is NOT eVisa. When the bearer of ETA is allowed entry into India by the Immigration Officer, it becomes eVisa. eVisa will be stamped on the Passport of the foreigner at the designated ICPs on production of valid ETA.

😊 Anything else you'd like to know?

A: ✅ **Is it true that I cannot travel to Iraq for work?**

Q: is possible to now my passport is ECR or ECNR

A: 💡 Look at the last page of your passport.
- If it has an endorsement saying “Emigration Check Required,” you are in the ECR category.
- If there is no such endorsement, or if it explicitly says "ECNR" (or refers to the holder as being in a non-ECR category), you do not need POE clearance for travel.

😊 Anything else you'd like to know?

[2026-11-12 12:47:43] (response time: 0.00 seconds = 0 minutes 0.00 seconds)
Q: what is eta ?

A: 📘 ETA is an Electronic Travel Authorization. It allows the bearer access into the issuing country (India). ETA is NOT eVisa. When the bearer of ETA is allowed entry into India by the Immigration Officer, it becomes eVisa. eVisa will be stamped on the Passport of the foreigner at the designated ICPs on production of valid ETA.

😊 Anything else you'd like to know?

* **Capital of Tamil Nadu:** சென்னை.
* **State symbols:** The state tree is the பனை மரம், the state animal is the நீலகிரி வரையாடு, the state bird is the மரகதப் புறா, and the state flower is the செங்காந்தள் மலர்.
* Authors: Thiruvalluvar, who wrote the Thirukkural, Ilango Adigal who wrote the Chilapathikaram, and Seethalai Saathanar who wrote the Manimekalai.
* Thirukkural details: It consists of 133 chapters, 1330 couplets, and is divided into three parts: Aram (virtue), Porul (wealth), and Kamam (love).

Q: What does page 1 say?

A: This page contains a set of general knowledge questions and answers in Tamil about Tamil Nadu and classical Tamil literature. 📄
Here is a summary of the information provided:
* **Capital of Tamil Nadu:** சென்னை (Chennai)
* **State Tree:** பனை மரம் (Palmyra tree)
* **State Animal:** நீலகிரி வரையாடு (Nilgiri tahr)
* **State Bird:** மரகதப் புறா (Emerald dove)
* **State Flower:** செங்காந்தள் மலர் (Red cotton flower)
* **Author of Thirukkural:** திருவள்ளுவர்
* **Thirukkural Structure:** It has **133** அதிகாரங்கள் (chapters) and **1330** குறள்கள் (couplets), divided into three பால்கள் (sections): அறத்துப்பால், பொருட்பால், and காமத்துப்பால்.
* **Author of Silappatikaram:** இளங்கோவடிகள்
* **Author of Manimekalai:** சீத்தலைச் சாத்தனார்
Hope that helps! 😊

| Ingestion | 1. Extract | pdfplumber extracts raw text and tables from PDF pages |
